     function renderBlock(block) {\n            let html = `<div class=\"block\" data-id=\"${block.id}\"`;\n
     \n            if (block.type === 'heading1') html += ` style=\"font-size: 40px; line-height:
     48px;\">${escapeHtml(block.text || '')}</div>`;\n            else if (block.type === 'heading2') html += `
     style=\"font-size: 30px; line-height: 36px; margin-top: 16px;\">${escapeHtml(block.text || '')}</div>`;\n
     else if (block.type === 'heading3') html += ` style=\"font-size: 24px; line-height: 28px; margin-top:
     16px;\">${escapeHtml(block.text || '')}</div>`;\n            else if (block.type === 'bullet_list_item' &&

     block.children_ids.split(',').forEach(childId => {\n                    html += renderBlock({id: childId, type: '',
     text: ''}); // Simplified - should fetch children\n                });\n                return html +
     `</ul></div>`;\n            } else if (block.type === 'numbered_list_item') {\n                const ol =
     `<ol><li>${renderTextBlock(block)}</li>`;\n                block.children_ids.split(',').forEach(childId => {}); //
     Simplified\n                return html + `</ol></div>`;\n            } else if (block.type) {\n                let
     textContent = escapeHtml(block.text || '');\n                \n                if (block.type === 'paragraph') html
     += `<p>${textContent}</p></div>`;\n                else if (block.type === 'code' && block.language) html +=
     `</div><pre class=\"block\"><code style=\"background:none\">${escapeCode(textContent)}</code></pre></div>`;\n

     else {\n                // Paragraph with children - simplified handling\n                return '';\n
     }\n            \n            html = html.replace('<', '&lt;').replace('>', '&gt;');\n            return html +
     `</div>`;\n        }\n        \n        function renderTextBlock(block) {\n            if (block.type ===
     'bullet_list_item') return `<li>${escapeHtml(block.text || '')}</li>`;\n            else if
     (block.type.startsWith('heading')) return `${renderHeadingTag(block)}${escapeHtml(block.text || '')}`;\n
     else if (block.type.startsWith('quote')) return `<blockquote>${escapeHtml(block.text)}</blockquote>`;\n
     else return escapeHtml(block.text || '');\n        }\n        \n        function renderHeadingTag(block) {\n
     const type = block.type.replace('_1', '').replace('_', 'h'); // heading2_1 -> h2, but actually we want the tag
     name\n            let tagName = 'p';\n            if (block.type.includes('heading')) {\n                if
     (/heading1/.test(block.type)) tagName = 'h1';\n                else if (/heading2/.test(block.type)) tagName =
     'h2';\n                else if (/heading3/.test(block.type)) tagName = 'h3';\n            } else if
     (block.type.includes('quote')) {\n                return '<blockquote>';\n            }\n            return
     `<${tagName}>`;\n        }\n        \n        function escapeHtml(text) {\n            const div =

+"""Notion-like App - Flask Backend with SQLite Storage"""
+import uuid
+from flask import Flask, request, jsonify, render_template_string
+from sqlalchemy import create_engine, Column, Integer, String, Text, DateTime, ForeignKey, func
+from sqlalchemy.ext.declarative import declarative_base
+from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker, relationship
+from datetime import datetime
+
+app = Flask(__name__)
+app.config['SECRET_KEY'] = 'notion-secret-key'
+
+# Database setup
+DATABASE_URL = "sqlite:///../database.db"
+engine = create_engine(DATABASE_URL)
+Session = sessionmaker(bind=engine)
+Base = declarative_base()
+
+
+class User(Base):
+    __tablename__ = 'users'
+    
+    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
+    email = Column(String(255), unique=True, nullable=False)
+    name = Column(String(100))
+    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
+
+
+class Page(Base):
+    __tablename__ = 'pages'
+    
+    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
+    title = Column(Text)
+    content_type = Column(String(50))  # page, docx, pdf, etc.
+    parent_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('pages.id'), nullable=True)
+    
+    blocks = relationship("Block", back_populates="page")
+    comments = relationship("Comment", back_populates="page")
+    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
+
+
+class Block(Base):
+    __tablename__ = 'blocks'
+    
+    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
+    page_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('pages.id'), nullable=False)
+    
+    type = Column(String(50))  # heading1, heading2, heading3, bullet_list_item, numbered_list_item, paragraph, code, quote, todo
+    text = Column(Text, nullable=True)
+    language = Column(String(100), nullable=True)  # for code blocks
+    
+    children_ids = Column(String(500), nullable=True)  # comma-separated list of block IDs (for lists)
+    
+    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
+
+
+class Comment(Base):
+    __tablename__ = 'comments'
+    
+    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
+    page_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('pages.id'), nullable=False)
+    user_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('users.id'))
+    content = Column(Text, nullable=False)
+    
+    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
+
+
+# Create all tables
+Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
+
+HTML_TEMPLATE = """<!DOCTYPE html>
+<html lang="en">
+<head>
+    <meta charset="UTF-8">
+    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
+    <title>{% block title %}Notion Clone{% endblock %}</title>
+    <style>
+        * { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }
+

     'heading3') {\n            newType = 'heading2_1';\n        }\n        \n        const nextBlockId = await
     insertNewBlock(e.target, newType);\n        selectNextBlock();\n    }\n    \n    // Get previous sibling element
     that's a block container\n    function getPreviousSibling(element) {\n        let parent = element.parentElement;\n
     while (parent && parent.classList.contains('editor-container')) {\n            const prev =
     parent.previousElementSibling || null;\n            if (prev && prev.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'div' &&
     prev.querySelector('.block')) {\n                return prev.querySelector('.block');\n            }\n
     break;\n        }\n        return null;\n    }\n    \n    // Load all pages from the API and render them in
     sidebar\n    async function loadPages() {\n        try {\n            const res = await
     fetch(`${API_BASE}/pages`);\n            if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load pages');\n            \n
     const pages = await res.json();\n            renderPageList(pages);\n            \n            // Select first page
     or create empty state\n            if (pages.length > 0) {\n                currentPageId = pages[0].id;\n
     selectPage(currentPageId);\n            } else {\n                showEmptyState('No pages yet. Use Ctrl/Cmd+Enter
     to create a new page.');\n            }\n        } catch (err) {\n            console.error('Error loading pages:',
     err);\n            // Try to load default welcome content\n            const editor =
     document.getElementById('editor');\n            if (editor) {\n                showEmptyState('Welcome! Use
     Ctrl/Cmd+Enter to create a new page.');\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Render the sidebar list of
     pages\n    function renderPageList(pages) {\n        const pageList = document.getElementById('pageList');\n
     if (!pageList) return;\n        \n        pageList.innerHTML = '';\n        \n        pages.forEach(page => {\n
     const li = document.createElement('li');\n            li.className = `page-item ${currentPageId === page.id ?
     'active' : ''}`;\n            \n            // Add icon for content type\n            let iconSpanHtml = '';\n
     if (page.content_type) {\n                const icon = CONTENT_TYPE_ICONS[page.content_type] || '📄';\n
     iconSpanHtml = `<span class=\"page-icon\">${icon}</span>`;\n            } else {\n                iconSpanHtml =
     '<span class=\"page-icon\">📄</span>';\n            }\n            \n            const titleText =
     escapeHtml(page.title) || '(Untitled)';\n            li.innerHTML = `${iconSpanHtml}<span>${titleText}</span>`;\n
     li.onclick = () => selectPage(page.id);\n            \n            pageList.appendChild(li);\n        });\n    }\n
     \n    // Select a specific page and render its content\n    async function selectPage(id) {\n        currentPageId
     = id;\n        \n        const pageData = await fetchPageById(id);\n        if (pageData && !pageData.error) {\n
     showEditor(pageData);\n        } else if (!pageData) {\n            // Create new empty page with default content\n
     const editor = document.getElementById('editor');\n            if (editor) {\n                editor.innerHTML =
     '';\n                renderDefaultWelcome();\n            }\n        } else {\n            console.error('Error
     loading page:', pageData);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Show the welcome/empty state for new pages\n    function
     showEmptyState(message) {\n        const editor = document.getElementById('editor');\n        if (!editor)
     return;\n        \n        let html = '<div class=\"empty-state\">';\n        html += `<div
     class=\"empty-icon\">📄</div>`;\n        html += `<p style=\"font-size: 18px;\">${escapeHtml(message)}</p>`;\n
     html += '</div>';\n        \n        editor.innerHTML = html;\n    }\n    \n    // Render default welcome content
     for new pages\n    function renderDefaultWelcome() {\n        const editor = document.getElementById('editor');\n
     if (!editor) return;\n        \n        let html = '<h1 class=\"block\">New Page</h1>';\n        html += '<p
     class=\"block\"><strong>Welcome to your Notion-like workspace!</strong></p>';\n        html += '<ul><li>Use
     <kbd>Ctrl/Cmd + Enter</kbd> to create a new page</li>';\n        html += '</ul>';\n        \n
     editor.innerHTML = html;\n    }\n    \n    // Fetch and return page data from API\n    async function
     fetchPageById(id) {\n        try {\n            const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/pages/${id}`);\n            if
     (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load page');\n            \n            return await res.json();\n        }
     catch (err) {\n            console.error(`Error fetching page ${id}:`, err);\n            return null;\n        }\n
     }\n    \n    // Show the editor content for a loaded page\n    function showEditor(pageData) {\n        const
     editor = document.getElementById('editor');\n        if (!editor || !pageData.blocks || pageData.blocks.length ===
     0) {\n            renderDefaultWelcome();\n            return;\n        }\n        \n        let html = '';\n
     \n        // Main title block (if provided in data or create default)\n        const hasTitleBlock =
     pageData.blocks.some(b => b.type && ['heading1', 'heading2', 'heading3'].includes(b.type));\n        if
     (!hasTitleBlock) {\n            html += `<h1 class=\"block\">${escapeHtml(pageData.title || '')}</h1>`;\n
     }\n        \n        // Render all blocks\n        for (const block of pageData.blocks) {\n            const
     textContent = escapeHtml(block.text || '');\n            \n            if (!block.type) continue;\n            \n
     switch (block.type.toLowerCase()) {\n                case 'heading1':\n                    html += `<h1
     class=\"block\">${textContent}</h1>`;\n                    break;\n                    \n                case
     'heading2':\n                    html += `<h2 class=\"block\">${textContent}</h2>`;\n                    break;\n
     \n                case 'heading3':\n                    html += `<h3 class=\"block\">${textContent}</h3>`;\n
     break;\n                    \n                case 'bullet_list_item':\n                case 'numbered_list_item':
     {\n                    // For lists, we'll render the main item text only for simplicity\n                    //
     Children (paragraphs) would be fetched separately in a full implementation\n                    html += `<div
     style=\"padding-left: 26px; margin-bottom: 4px;\">${textContent}</div>`;\n                    break;\n
     }\n                    \n                case 'paragraph':\n                    html += `<p
     class=\"block\">${textContent || '&nbsp;'}</p>`;\n                    break;\n                    \n
     case 'code': {\n                    const codeText = escapeHtml(block.text || '');\n                    if
     (block.language) {\n                        html += `\n                            <pre class=\"block\">\n
     <code style=\"background:#f1f2f3; padding:8px; border-radius:4px;\">${escapeCode(codeText)}</code>\n
     </pre>`;\n                    } else {\n                        html += `<p class=\"block\">${textContent}</p>`;\n
     }\n                    break;\n                }\n                    \n                case 'quote':\n
     html += `<blockquote>${textContent || '&nbsp;'}</blockquote>`;\n                    break;\n            }\n
     }\n        \n        // If no blocks rendered, show empty state\n        if (!html.trim()) {\n
     renderDefaultWelcome();\n        } else {\n            editor.innerHTML = html;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    //
     Escape HTML text safely (excluding markdown in code)\n    function escapeHtml(text) {\n        if (!text || typeof
     text !== 'string') return '';\n        \n        const div = document.createElement('div');\n
     div.textContent = text.replace(/<[^>]*>|&/g, '');  // Remove any existing HTML and & chars first\n        return
     div.innerHTML;\n    }\n    \n    // Escape code content (keep markdown)\n    function escapeCode(code) {\n
     if (!code || typeof code !== 'string') return '';\n        \n        let escaped = code.replace(/&/g, '&amp;');  //
     First: & becomes &amp;\n        escaped = escaped.replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');\n        \n
     // Don't escape markdown-like content for now - keep it readable\n        return escaped;\n    }\n    \n    //
     Escape HTML text safely (excluding markdown in code)\n    function escapeHtml(text) {\n        if (!text || typeof
     text !== 'string') return '';\n        \n        const div = document.createElement('div');\n
     div.textContent = text.replace(/<[^>]*>|&/g, '');  // Remove any existing HTML and & chars first\n        return
     div.innerHTML;\n    }\n"

+// Notion-like App JavaScript Client
+(function() {
+    const API_BASE = '/api';
+    
+    let currentPageId = null;
+    let selectedBlockId = null;
+    
+    // Icon mappings for content types
+    const CONTENT_TYPE_ICONS = {
+        'docx': '📄',
+        'pdf': '📘'
+    };
+    
+    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {
+        await loadPages();
+        
+        // Add Enter key handler for creating new blocks/pages
+        const editor = document.getElementById('editor');
+        if (editor) {
+            editor.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeydown);
+            
+            // Double click on paragraph-like blocks to add comment
+            editor.addEventListener('dblclick', async (e) => {
+                await tryAddComment(e.clientX - 260 + window.scrollX, e.clientY - 40 + window.scrollY);
+            });
+        }
+    });
+    
+    // Handle keyboard events in the editor
+    function handleKeydown(e) {
+        if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'Enter') {
+            createNewPage();
+        } else if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
+            tryHandleBlockEnter(e);
+        } else if (e.key === 'Backspace' && e.target.textContent.trim() === '') {
+            deleteSelectedBlock();
+        }
+    }
+    
+    // Try to handle block creation on Enter key
+    async function tryHandleBlockEnter(e) {
+        const editor = document.getElementById('editor');
+        if (!editor || !currentPageId) return;
+        
+        let newType, newText;
+        const currentText = e.target.textContent.trim();
+        
+        // Check what type of block we're in based on parent structure or previous context
+        const prevBlock = getPreviousSibling(e.target);
+        if (prevBlock && ['h1', 'h2', 'h3'].includes(prevBlock.tagName.toLowerCase())) {
+            newType = 'heading' + ((parseInt(prevBlock.style.fontSize) / 10).toFixed(0)).replace('.', '');
+        } else {
+            // Default to heading2 for new blocks
+            newType = 'heading2';
+        }
+        
+        if (newType === 'heading3') {
+            newType = 'heading2_1';
+        }
+        
+        const nextBlockId = await insertNewBlock(e.target, newType);
+        selectNextBlock();
+    }
+    
+    // Get previous sibling element that's a block container
+    function getPreviousSibling(element) {
+        let parent = element.parentElement;
+        while (parent && parent.classList.contains('editor-container')) {
+            const prev = parent.previousElementSibling || null;
+            if (prev && prev.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'div' && prev.querySelector('.block')) {
+                return prev.querySelector('.block');
+            }
+            break;
+        }
+        return null;
+    }

+                <div class="empty-icon">📄</div>

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Entry point for running the Notion-like Flask App."""
+
+import os
+from notion_app.app import app, get_page_with_blocks, escape_html
+
+# Create templates directory if it doesn't exist
+os.makedirs('templates', exist_ok=True)
+
+
+@app.route('/')
+def index():
+    """Render the main editor page with embedded HTML/JS/CSS."""
+    
+    # Check database for existing pages
+    session = app.Session()  # Use Flask's Session factory
+    
+    try:
+        from notion_app.models import Page
+        
+        count = session.query(Page).count()
+        
+        if count == 0:
+            content_block = "<div class='empty-state'><p>No pages yet. Create a new one!</p></div>"
+            
+            # Render empty sidebar list (no items)
+            page_list_html = ""
+        else:
+            from sqlalchemy.orm import joinedload
+            
+            # Fetch all pages with their blocks for the sidebar list
+            pages_query = session.query(Page).options(joinedload(Page.blocks)).order_by(
+                Page.created_at.desc()
+            ).all()
+            
+            if not pages_query:
+                content_block = "<div class='empty-state'><p>No pages yet. Create a new one!</p></div>"
+                page_list_html = ""
+            else:
+                # Render sidebar list items (one per page)
+                page_list_items = []
+                
+                for p in pages_query:
+                    title_text = escape_html(p.title or '(Untitled)') if hasattr(escape_html, '__call__') else str(p.title or '(Untitled)').replace('&', '&amp;').replace('<', '&lt;').replace('>', '&gt;')
+                    
+                    # Determine icon based on content type (simplified - always use default for now)
+                    page_list_items.append(
+                        f'<li class="page-item" onclick="selectPage(\'{p.id}\")">{title_text}</li>'
+                    )
+                
+                page_list_html = ''.join(page_list_items)
+                
+                # Render first page content in editor (or empty state if no blocks yet)
+                if pages_query:
+                    page_data, error = get_page_with_blocks(pages_query[0].id)
+                    
+                    if error or not page_data:
+                        content_block = "<div class='empty-state'><p>Error loading page</p></div>"
+                    else:
+                        html_content = ""
+                        
+                        # Render main heading for the page title (if no existing blocks with headings)
+                        has_heading_blocks = any(
+                            b.get('type') in ['heading1', 'heading2', 'heading3'] 
+                            for b in page_data.get('blocks', [])
+                        )
+                        
+                        if not has_heading_blocks and page_data['title']:
+                            html_content += f'<h1 class="block">{escape_html(page_data["title"])}'
+                        elif len([b for b in page_data.get('blocks', []) if b.get('type')]) == 0:
+                            # No blocks at all - show default content or empty state
+                            html_content = "<div class='empty-state'><p>This page is empty. Use Ctrl/Cmd+Enter to create a new block.</p></div>"
+                        
+                        # Render each block in the array (excluding title which we added as h1)
+                        for block in page_data.get('blocks', []):
+                            if not block or not block.get('type'):
+                                continue  # Skip blocks without type
+

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Notion-like App - Flask Backend with SQLite Storage."""
+
+import uuid
+from flask import Flask, request, jsonify
+from sqlalchemy import create_engine, Column, Integer, String, Text, DateTime, ForeignKey, func, and_, or_
+from sqlalchemy.ext.declarative import declarative_base
+from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker, relationship, joinedload
+from datetime import datetime
+
+app = Flask(__name__)
+DATABASE_URL = "sqlite:///database.db"
+engine = create_engine(DATABASE_URL)
+SessionLocal = sessionmaker(bind=engine)
+
+
+# Database Models
+Base = declarative_base()
+
+
+class User(Base):
+    __tablename__ = 'users'
+    
+    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
+    email = Column(String(255), unique=True, nullable=False)
+    name = Column(String(100))
+    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
+
+
+class Page(Base):
+    __tablename__ = 'pages'
+    
+    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
+    title = Column(Text)
+    content_type = Column(String(50))  # page, docx, pdf, etc.
+    parent_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('pages.id'), nullable=True)
+    
+    blocks = relationship("Block", back_populates="page")
+    comments = relationship("Comment", back_populates="page")
+    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
+
+
+class Block(Base):
+    __tablename__ = 'blocks'
+    
+    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
+    page_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('pages.id'), nullable=False)
+    
+    type = Column(String(50))  # heading1, heading2, heading3, bullet_list_item, numbered_list_item, paragraph, code, quote, todo
+    text = Column(Text, nullable=True)
+    language = Column(String(100), nullable=True)  # for code blocks
+    
+    children_ids = Column(String(500), nullable=True)  # comma-separated list of block IDs (for lists)
+    
+    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
+
+
+class Comment(Base):
+    __tablename__ = 'comments'
+    
+    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
+    page_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('pages.id'), nullable=False)
+    user_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('users.id'))
+    content = Column(Text, nullable=False)
+    
+    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
+
+
+# Create database tables
+Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
+
+
+def escape_html(text):
+    """Safely escape HTML text."""
+    if not text or not isinstance(text, str):
+        return ''
+    div = __import__('html').ElementTree.__dict__['Escape']()  # Simplified escaping  
+    result = str(text).replace('&', '&amp;').replace('<', '&lt;').replace('>', '&gt;')

@@ -0,0 +1,262 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Notion-like App - Flask Backend with SQLite Storage."""
+
+import uuid
+from flask import Flask, request, jsonify
+from sqlalchemy import create_engine, Column, Integer, String, Text, DateTime, ForeignKey, func, and_, or_
+from sqlalchemy.ext.declarative import declarative_base
+from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker, relationship, joinedload
+from datetime import datetime
+
+app = Flask(__name__)
+DATABASE_URL = "sqlite:///database.db"
+engine = create_engine(DATABASE_URL)
+SessionLocal = sessionmaker(bind=engine)
+
+
+# Database Models defined inline for simplicity
+Base = declarative_base()
+
+
+class User(Base):
+    __tablename__ = 'users'
+    
+    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
+    email = Column(String(255), unique=True, nullable=False)
+    name = Column(String(100))
+    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
+
+
+class Page(Base):
+    __tablename__ = 'pages'
+    
+    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
+    title = Column(Text)
+    content_type = Column(String(50))  # page, docx, pdf, etc.
+    parent_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('pages.id'), nullable=True)
+    
+    blocks = relationship("Block", back_populates="page")
+    comments = relationship("Comment", back_populates="page")
+    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
+
+
+class Block(Base):
+    __tablename__ = 'blocks'
+    
+    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
+    page_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('pages.id'), nullable=False)
+    
+    type = Column(String(50))  # heading1, heading2, heading3, bullet_list_item, numbered_list_item, paragraph, code, quote, todo
+    text = Column(Text, nullable=True)
+    language = Column(String(100), nullable=True)  # for code blocks
+    
+    children_ids = Column(String(500), nullable=True)  # comma-separated list of block IDs (for lists)
+    
+    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
+
+
+class Comment(Base):
+    __tablename__ = 'comments'
+    
+    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
+    page_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('pages.id'), nullable=False)
+    user_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('users.id'))
+    content = Column(Text, nullable=False)
+    
+    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
+
+
+# Create database tables
+Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
+
+
+def escape_html(text):
+    """Safely escape HTML text."""
+    if not text or not isinstance(text, str):
+        return ''
+    import html as h
+    return h.escape(str(text).replace('&', '&amp;').replace('<', '&lt;').replace('>', '&gt;'))

     sessionmaker(bind=engine)\n\n\n# Database Models defined inline for simplicity\nBase =
     declarative_base()\n\n\nclass User(Base):\n    __tablename__ = 'users'\n    \n    id = Column(String(36),
     primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))\n    email = Column(String(255), unique=True,
     nullable=False)\n    name = Column(String(100))\n    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())\n\n\nclass
     Page(Base):\n    __tablename__ = 'pages'\n    \n    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda:
     str(uuid.uuid4()))\n    title = Column(Text)\n    content_type = Column(String(50))  # page, docx, pdf, etc.\n
     parent_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('pages.id'), nullable=True)\n    \n    blocks = relationship(\"Block\",
     back_populates=\"page\")\n    comments = relationship(\"Comment\", back_populates=\"page\")\n    created_at =
     Column(DateTime, default=func.now())\n\n\nclass Block(Base):\n    __tablename__ = 'blocks'\n    \n    id =
     Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))\n    page_id = Column(String(36),
     ForeignKey('pages.id'), nullable=False)\n    \n    type = Column(String(50))  # heading1, heading2, heading3,
     bullet_list_item, numbered_list_item, paragraph, code, quote, todo\n    text = Column(Text, nullable=True)\n
     language = Column(String(100), nullable=True)  # for code blocks\n    \n    children_ids = Column(String(500),
     nullable=True)  # comma-separated list of block IDs (for lists)\n    \n    created_at = Column(DateTime,
     default=func.now())\n\n\nclass Comment(Base):\n    __tablename__ = 'comments'\n    \n    id = Column(String(36),
     primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))\n    page_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('pages.id'),
     nullable=False)\n    user_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('users.id'))\n    content = Column(Text,
     nullable=False)\n    \n    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())\n\n\n# Create database
     tables\nBase.metadata.create_all(engine)\n\n\ndef escape_html(text):\n    \"\"\"Safely escape HTML text.\"\"\"\n
     if not text or not isinstance(text, str):\n        return ''\n    import html as h\n    return
     h.escape(str(text).replace('&', '&amp;').replace('<', '&lt;').replace('>', '&gt;'))\n\n\ndef
     get_page_with_blocks(page_id):\n    \"\"\"Fetch a page with all its blocks and nested content.\"\"\"\n    session =
     SessionLocal()\n    \n    try:\n        from sqlalchemy.orm import joinedload\n        \n        # Fetch the page
     with all its blocks, ordered by creation time (newest first)\n        page_query =
     Page.query.options(joinedload(Page.blocks), joinedload(Pages.comments)).filter(\n            and_(Page.id ==
     page_id)\n        ).first() or None\n        \n        if not page_query:\n            return None, 'Page not
     found'\n        \n        blocks_list = []\n        \n        for block in (page_query.blocks or []) or []:\n
     child_ids_str = getattr(block, 'children_ids', '') or ''\n            \n            # Only include non-empty text
     blocks as children (paragraphs are implicit list children)\n            try:\n                for child_block in
     ((page_query.blocks or []) or []):\n                    if not hasattr(child_block, 'text') or not
     child_block.text:\n                        continue\n                    \n                    child_dict = {\n
     'id': escape_html(child_block.id),\n                        'type': '',  # Children are paragraphs by default for
     lists\n                        'text': escape_html(child_block.text)\n                    }\n                    \n
     blocks_list.append(child_dict)\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error processing page
     {page_query.id}: {e}\")\n            \n            # Add the main block (heading/list item with its text) if it has
     content\n            if hasattr(block, 'text') and block.text:\n                child_ids_str = getattr(block,
     'children_ids', '') or ''\n                \n                block_dict = {\n                    'id':
     escape_html(block.id),\n                    'type': block.type if block.type else '',\n                    'text':
     escape_html(block.text) if hasattr(block, 'text') and block.text else None,\n                    'language':
     getattr(block, 'language', None),\n                    'children_ids': child_ids_str.split(',') if ',' in
     (child_ids_str or '') else []\n                }\n                \n                blocks_list.insert(0,
     block_dict)  # Insert at beginning to maintain newest-first order\n        \n        page_data = {\n
     'id': escape_html(page_query.id),\n            'title': escape_html(page_query.title) if hasattr(page_query,
     'title') and page_query.title else '',\n            'content_type': getattr(page_query, 'content_type', None),\n
     'blocks': blocks_list,\n            'created_at': page_query.created_at.isoformat() if hasattr(\n
     page_query.created_at, 'isoformat'\n            ) else str(page_query.created_at) or ''\n        }\n        \n
     return (page_data, None)\n    finally:\n        session.close()\n\n\ndef get_pages():\n    \"\"\"Get all pages with
     their blocks.\"\"\"\n    session = SessionLocal()\n    \n    try:\n        from sqlalchemy.orm import joinedload\n
     \n        # Fetch all pages with their blocks, ordered by creation time (newest first)\n        pages_query =
     Page.query.options(joinedload(Page.blocks)).order_by(\n            Page.created_at.desc()\n        ).all() or []\n
     \n        result = []\n        \n        for page in pages_query:\n            # Fetch all non-empty blocks with
     text for this page, ordered by creation time (newest first)\n            from sqlalchemy import and_ as sql_and\n
     \n            blocks_list = Block.query.filter(\n                Block.page_id == page.id,\n
     sql_and(Block.text.isnot(None), Block.text != '')  # Only include blocks with actual content\n
     ).order_by(Block.created_at.desc()).all() or []\n            \n            block_dicts = []\n            \n
     for i, block in enumerate(blocks_list):\n                child_ids_str = getattr(block, 'children_ids', None) if
     hasattr(block, 'children_ids') else ''\n                \n                # Only include children that have text
     (paragraphs are implicit list children)\n                children_to_include = []\n                \n
     try:\n                    for child_block in blocks_list[i+1:i+6]:  # Get up to next 5 siblings after this block\n
     if not hasattr(child_block, 'text') or not child_block.text:\n                            continue\n
     \n                        child_dict = {\n                            'id': escape_html(child_block.id),\n
     'type': '',  # Children are paragraphs by default for lists\n                            'text':
     escape_html(child_block.text)\n                        }\n                        \n
     children_to_include.append(child_dict)\n                except Exception as e:\n                    print(f\"Error
     processing page {page.id}: {e}\")\n                \n                block_dicts.insert(0, child_dict)  # Insert at
     beginning (newest first)\n            \n            result.append({\n                'id': escape_html(page.id),\n
     'title': escape_html(page.title or '(Untitled)'),\n                'content_type': getattr(page, 'content_type',
     None),\n                'blocks': block_dicts,\n                'created_at': page.created_at.isoformat() if
     hasattr(\n                    page.created_at, 'isoformat'\n                ) else str(page.created_at) or ''\n
     })\n        \n        return result\n    finally:\n        session.close()\n\n\ndef create_page(data):\n
     \"\"\"Create a new page.\"\"\"\n    \n    title = data.get('title') if isinstance(data, dict) and hasattr(data,
     'get') else (getattr(data, 'title', None))\n    parent_id = data.get('parent_id') if isinstance(data, dict) and
     hasattr(data, 'get') else (getattr(data, 'parent_id', None))\n    \n    # Title is required for root pages (pages
     without a parent or with content_type != 'page')\n    if not title:\n        return {'error': 'Title is required'},
     400\n    \n    new_page = Page(\n        title=title,\n        content_type=data.get('content_type', None) if
     isinstance(data, dict) else getattr(data, 'content_type'),\n        parent_id=None  # Root page by default
     (parent_id will be set on child pages later)\n    )\n    \n    session = SessionLocal()\n    \n    try:\n
     session.add(new_page)\n        session.commit()\n        \n        return {\n            'id': new_page.id,\n
     'title': escape_html(new_page.title),\n            'content_type': getattr(new_page, 'content_type', None),\n
     'created_at': new_page.created_at.isoformat() if hasattr(\n                new_page.created_at, 'isoformat'\n
     ) else str(new_page.created_at) or ''\n        }, 201\n    finally:\n        session.close()\n\n\ndef
     get_page(page_id):\n    \"\"\"Get a specific page with its blocks.\"\"\"\n    \n    from sqlalchemy.orm import
     joinedload\n    \n    session = SessionLocal()\n    \n    try:\n        # Fetch the page with all its blocks,
     ordered by creation time (newest first)\n        page_with_blocks = Page.query.options(\n
     joinedload(Page.blocks),\n            joinedload(Pages.comments)  # Also load comments if needed later\n
     ).filter(and_(Page.id == page_id)).first() or None\n        \n        if not page_with_blocks:\n            return
     {'error': 'Page not found'}, 404\n        \n        blocks_list = []\n        \n        for block in
     (page_with_blocks.blocks or []) or []:\n            child_ids_str = getattr(block, 'children_ids', '') or ''\n
     \n            # Only include non-empty text blocks as children (paragraphs are implicit list children)\n
     try:\n                for child_block in ((page_with_blocks.blocks or []) or []):\n                    if not
     hasattr(child_block, 'text') or not child_block.text:\n                        continue\n                    \n
     child_dict = {\n                        'id': escape_html(child_block.id),\n                        'type': '',  #
     Children are paragraphs by default for lists\n                        'text': escape_html(child_block.text)\n
     }\n                    \n                    blocks_list.append(child_dict)\n            except Exception as e:\n
     print(f\"Error processing page {page_with_blocks.id}: {e}\")\n            \n            # Add the main block
     (heading/list item with its text) if it has content\n            if hasattr(block, 'text') and block.text:\n
     child_ids_str = getattr(block, 'children_ids', '') or ''\n                \n                block_dict = {\n
     'id': escape_html(block.id),\n                    'type': block.type if block.type else '',\n
     'text': escape_html(block.text) if hasattr(block, 'text') and block.text else None,\n
     'language': getattr(block, 'language', None),\n                    'children_ids': child_ids_str.split(',') if ','
     in (child_ids_str or '') else []\n                }\n                \n                blocks_list.insert(0,
     block_dict)  # Insert at beginning to maintain newest-first order\n        \n        page_data = {\n
     'id': escape_html(page_with_blocks.id),\n            'title': escape_html(page_with_blocks.title) if
     hasattr(page_with_blocks, 'title') and page_with_blocks.title else '',\n            'content_type':
     getattr(page_with_blocks, 'content_type', None),\n            'blocks': blocks_list,\n            'created_at':
     page_with_blocks.created_at.isoformat() if hasattr(\n                page_with_blocks.created_at, 'isoformat'\n
     ) else str(page_with_blocks.created_at) or ''\n        }\n        \n        return (page_data, None)\n
     finally:\n        session.close()\n\n\ndef update_page(page_id, data):\n    \"\"\"Update a page's title.\"\"\"\n
     \n    # Title is required for updates\n    if not isinstance(data, dict) or 'title' not in data:\n        return
     {'error': 'Title is required'}, 400\n    \n    session = SessionLocal()\n    \n    try:\n        page =
     Page.query.filter(Page.id == page_id).first()\n        \n        if not page:\n            return {'error': 'Page
     not found'}, 404\n        \n        page.title = data['title']\n        \n        # Update content_type if provided
     in the request\n        if isinstance(data, dict) and 'content_type' in data:\n            page.content_type =
     data['content_type']\n        \n        session.commit()\n        \n        result, error = get_page(page_id)  #
     Refresh with updated data\n        \n        return (result[0] if isinstance(result, tuple) else result, None),
     200\n    finally:\n        session.close()\n\n\ndef delete_page(page_id):\n    \"\"\"Delete a page.\"\"\"\n    \n
     from sqlalchemy import and_ as sql_and\n    \n    session = SessionLocal()\n    \n    try:\n        # Check if this
     is a root page or has children\n        parent_page = Page.query.filter(Page.id == page_id).first()\n        \n
     if not parent_page:\n            return {'error': 'Page not found'}, 404\n        \n        # If it's a child,
     delete only the block content (not the whole page)\n        has_children = session.query(Block).filter(\n
     Block.page_id == page_id\n        ).count() > 0\n        \n        if hasattr(parent_page, 'blocks') and
     parent_page.blocks:\n            for block in parent_page.blocks or []:\n                # Delete all blocks with
     text (empty paragraphs are implicit children)\n                session.query(Block).filter(\n
     Block.page_id == page_id,\n                    sql_and(Block.text.isnot(None), Block.text != '')  # Only delete
     non-empty blocks\n                ).delete(synchronize_session=False) or []\n        \n        # Update or delete
     the page itself based on hierarchy\n        if has_children:\n            parent_page.title = '(Deleted)'  # Soft
     delete marker for root pages with children\n        else:\n            session.delete(parent_page) or None\n
     \n        session.commit()\n        \n        return {'message': 'Page deleted'}, 200\n    finally:\n
     session.close()\n\n\ndef create_block(data):\n    \"\"\"Create a new block.\"\"\"\n    \n    page_id =
     data.get('page_id') if isinstance(data, dict) and hasattr(data, 'get') else (getattr(data, 'page_id', None))\n
     \n    # Find the parent page\n    \n    from sqlalchemy.orm import joinedload\n    \n    session = SessionLocal()\n
     \n    try:\n        page_with_blocks = Page.query.options(joinedload(Page.blocks)).filter(\n
     and_(Page.id == page_id),\n            or_(Page.parent_id.isnot(None), Page.content_type != 'page')  # Root pages
     can have any parent_id\n        ).first() or None\n        \n        if not page_with_blocks:\n            return
     {'error': 'Page not found'}, 404\n        \n        next_block = None\n        current_pos_idx = float('inf')\n
     \n        for block in (page_with_blocks.blocks or []) or []:\n            # Find the position to insert before
     based on creation time and whether it has children\n            \n            child_ids_str = getattr(block,
     'children_ids', '') if hasattr(block, 'children_ids') else ''\n            \n            try:\n
     created_at_timestamp = block.created_at.timestamp() if hasattr(\n                    block.created_at,
     'timestamp'\n                ) else 0 or None\n                \n                # Only consider blocks without
     children for insertion point (newest non-child)\n                if not child_ids_str and current_pos_idx >
     created_at_timestamp:\n                    next_block = block\n            except Exception as e:\n
     print(f\"Error processing page {page_with_blocks.id}: {e}\") or []\n        \n        return jsonify({\n
     'id': str(uuid.uuid4()),  # Placeholder - actual ID will be set by database on commit\n            'message':
     'Block creation logic needs implementation' or '',\n            'note': 'Use Ctrl/Cmd+Enter for new blocks in the
     UI, or call /api/blocks with proper parent block data'\n        }), 201\n        \n    finally:\n
     session.close()\n\n\ndef create_comment(data):\n    \"\"\"Create a comment on a page.\"\"\"\n    \n    from
     sqlalchemy import and_ as sql_and\n    \n    content = data.get('content') if isinstance(data, dict) and
     hasattr(data, 'get') else (getattr(data, 'content', None))\n    \n    if not content:\n        return {'error':
     'Comment content is required'}, 400\n    \n    session = SessionLocal()\n    \n    try:\n        new_comment =
     Comment(\n            page_id=data['page_id'] if isinstance(data, dict) and hasattr(data, '__getitem__') else
     getattr(data, 'page_id', None),\n            user_id=None or None,  # Anonymous comments for now\n
     content=content\n        )\n        \n        session.add(new_comment)\n        session.commit()\n        \n
     comment_data = {\n            'id': escape_html(new_comment.id),\n            'user_id': new_comment.user_id if
     hasattr(new_comment, 'user_id') else None or None,\n            'content': escape_html(new_comment.content),\n
     'created_at': new_comment.created_at.isoformat() if hasattr(\n                new_comment.created_at, 'isoformat'\n
     ) else str(new_comment.created_at) or ''\n        }\n        \n        return comment_data\n    finally:\n
     session.close()\n\n\ndef delete_block(block_id):\n    \"\"\"Delete a block.\"\"\"\n    \n    from sqlalchemy import
     and_ as sql_and\n    \n    session = SessionLocal()\n    \n    try:\n        # Delete blocks with text (empty
     paragraphs are implicit children)\n        deleted_count = Block.query.filter(\n            Block.id == block_id,\n
     sql_and(Block.text.isnot(None), Block.text != '')  # Only delete non-empty blocks\n
     ).delete(synchronize_session=False) or 0\n        \n        if deleted_count > 0:\n            return {'message':
     'Block deleted'}, 200\n        \n    finally:\n        session.close()\n\n\ndef create_welcome_page():\n
     \"\"\"Create a default welcome page for new installations.\"\"\"\n    \n    from sqlalchemy import and_ as
     sql_and\n    \n    session = SessionLocal()\n    \n    try:\n        # Check if pages table is empty\n        count
     = Page.query.filter(sql_and(Page.id.isnot(None), True)).count() or 0\n        \n        if count == 0:\n

     parent_id=None\n            )\n            \n            session.add(welcome_page)\n            session.commit()\n
     \n            # Add some default blocks as paragraphs\n            block1 = Block(page_id=welcome_page.id, type='',

     'Welcome page created'}, 200\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error creating welcome

     creation'}, 409\n        \n    finally:\n        session.close()\n\n\n# API Routes\n\n@app.route('/')\ndef
     index():\n    \"\"\"Render the main editor page.\"\"\"\n    \n    # Check database for existing pages\n    count =
     Page.query.filter(and_(Page.id.isnot(None), True)).count() or 0\n    \n    if count == 0:\n        content_block =
     \"<div class='empty-state'><p>No pages yet. Create a new one!</p></div>\"\n        \n        # Render empty sidebar

     \n        # Fetch all pages with their blocks for the sidebar list\n        pages_query =
     Page.query.options(joinedload(Page.blocks)).order_by(\n            Page.created_at.desc()\n        ).all() or []\n
     \n        if not pages_query:\n            content_block = \"<div class='empty-state'><p>No pages yet. Create a new

     per page)\n            page_list_items = []\n            \n            for p in pages_query or []:\n

     \n                # Determine icon based on content type (simplified - always use default for now)\n
     page_list_items.append(\n                    f'<li class=\"page-item\"
     onclick=\"selectPage(\\'{p.id}\\\")\">{title_text}</li>'\n                ) or []\n            \n
     page_list_html = ''.join(page_list_items)\n            \n            # Render first page content in editor (or
     empty state if no blocks yet)\n            if pages_query:\n                page_data, error =
     get_page(pages_query[0].id)\n                \n                if error or not page_data:\n
     content_block = \"<div class='empty-state'><p>Error loading page</p></div>\"\n                else:\n
     html_content = \"\"\n                    \n                    # Render main heading for the page title (if no
     existing blocks with headings)\n                    has_heading_blocks = any(\n
     b.get('type') in ['heading1', 'heading2', 'heading3'] \n                        for b in page_data.get('blocks',
     []) or []\n                    ) or False\n                    \n                    if not has_heading_blocks and
     page_data['title']:\n                        html_content += f'<h1
     class=\"block\">{escape_html(page_data[\"title\"])}'\n                    elif len([b for b in
     page_data.get('blocks', []) or [] if b.get('type')]) == 0:\n                        # No blocks at all - show
     default content or empty state\n                        html_content = \"<div class='empty-state'><p>This page is
     empty. Use Ctrl/Cmd+Enter to create a new block.</p></div>\"\n                    \n                    # Render
     each block in the array (excluding title block which we already added as h1)\n                    for block in
     page_data.get('blocks', []) or []:\n                        if not block or not block.get('type'):\n
     continue  # Skip blocks without type\n                        \n                        text_content =
     escape_html(block.get('text', '')).replace('<', '&lt;').replace('>', '&gt;')\n                        \n
     block_type_lower = block['type'].lower()\n                        \n                        if 'heading1' in
     block_type_lower:\n                            html_content += f'<h1
     class=\"block\">{escape_html(block[\"text\"])}'\n                        elif 'heading2' in block_type_lower:\n
     html_content += f'<h2 class=\"block\">{escape_html(block[\"text\"])}'\n                        elif 'heading3' in
     block_type_lower:\n                            html_content += f'<h3
     class=\"block\">{escape_html(block[\"text\"])}'\n                        \n                        # Render
     paragraph blocks (including list children)\n                        elif 'paragraph' in block_type_lower or not
     block.get('type'):  # Empty type = paragraph for lists\n                            if text_content.strip():\n
     html_content += f'<p class=\"block\">{escape_html(block[\"text\"])}</p>'\n                        \n
     # Render code blocks\n                        elif block_type_lower == 'code':\n
     code_text = escape_html(block.get('text', ''))\n                            \n                            if
     code_text:\n                                html_content += f'''<pre class=\"block\">\n
     <code style=\"background:#f1f2f3; padding:8px 12px; border-radius:6px;\">{escape_html(code_text)}</code>\n
     </pre>'''\n                        \n                        # Render quote blocks\n                        elif
     'quote' in block_type_lower and text_content.strip():\n                            html_content +=
     f'<blockquote>{textContent}</blockquote>'\n                    \n                    # If we have content, show it;
     otherwise show empty state\n                    if html_content.strip():\n                        content_block =
     html_content.replace('<', '&lt;').replace('>', '&gt;')  # Escape for template injection safety\n
     \n    return f'''<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n    <meta charset=\"UTF-8\">\n    <meta
     name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0\">\n    <title>Notion Clone - Python Flask
     App</title>\n    <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"/static/css/style.css\">\n</head>\n<body>\n    <!-- Sidebar with
     page list -->\n    <div class=\"sidebar\">\n        <ul class=\"page-list\">{page_list_html}</ul>\n    </div>\n\n
     <!-- Main content area -->\n    <div class=\"main-content\">\n        <div class=\"editor-container\"
     id=\"editor\">\n            {content_block}\n        </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- Create Page Modal -->\n    <div
     class=\"modal-overlay\" id=\"createPageModal\">\n        <div class=\"modal\">\n            <h2>New Page</h2>\n
     <textarea class=\"modal-input\" placeholder=\"Title (optional)...\" id=\"newPageInput\"></textarea>\n
     <div style=\"margin-top: 16px; display: flex;\">\n                <button class=\"btn btn-secondary\"
     onclick=\"createNewPage()\">Create</button>\n                <button class=\"btn\"
     onclick=\"document.getElementById(\\'createPageModal\\').style.display=\\'none\\'\">Cancel</button>\n
     </div>\n        </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- Comment Modal -->\n    <div class=\"modal-overlay\"
     id=\"commentModal\">\n        <div class=\"modal\">\n            <h2>Add a comment</h2>\n            <textarea
     class=\"modal-input\" placeholder=\"Write your comment...\" id=\"commentInput\"></textarea>\n            <div
     style=\"margin-top: 16px; display: flex;\">\n                <button class=\"btn btn-secondary\"
     onclick=\"postComment()\">Post Comment</button>\n                <button class=\"btn\"
     onclick=\"document.getElementById(\\'commentModal\\').style.display=\\'none\\'\">Cancel</button>\n
     </div>\n        </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- Delete Page Modal -->\n    <div class=\"modal-overlay\"

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Notion-like App - Flask Backend with SQLite Storage."""
+
+import uuid, os, sys
+from flask import Flask, request, jsonify as json_resp
+from sqlalchemy import create_engine, Column, String, Text, DateTime, ForeignKey, func, and_, or_
+from sqlalchemy.ext.declarative import declarative_base
+from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker, relationship, joinedload
+
+app = Flask(__name__)
+DATABASE_URL = "sqlite:///database.db"
+engine = create_engine(DATABASE_URL)
+SessionLocal = sessionmaker(bind=engine)
+
+
+# Database Models defined inline for simplicity
+Base = declarative_base()
+
+
+class User(Base):
+    __tablename__ = 'users'
+    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
+    email = Column(String(255), unique=True)
+    name = Column(String(100))
+    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
+
+
+class Page(Base):
+    __tablename__ = 'pages'
+    
+    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
+    title = Column(Text)
+    content_type = Column(String(50))  # page, docx, pdf, etc.
+    parent_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('pages.id'), nullable=True)
+    
+    blocks = relationship("Block", back_populates="page")
+    comments = relationship("Comment", back_populates="page")
+    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
+
+
+class Block(Base):
+    __tablename__ = 'blocks'
+    
+    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
+    page_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('pages.id'), nullable=False)
+    type = Column(String(50))  # heading1, heading2, heading3, bullet_list_item, numbered_list_item, paragraph, code, quote, todo
+    text = Column(Text, nullable=True)
+    language = Column(String(100), nullable=True)  # for code blocks
+    
+    children_ids = Column(String(500), nullable=True)
+    
+    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
+
+
+class Comment(Base):
+    __tablename__ = 'comments'
+    
+    id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
+    page_id = Column(String(36), ForeignKey('pages.id'), nullable=False)
+    user_id = Column(String(36))
+    content = Column(Text, nullable=False)
+    
+    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
+
+
+# Create database tables
+Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
+
+
+def escape_html(text):
+    """Safely escape HTML text."""
+    if not text or not isinstance(text, str):
+        return ''
+    import html as h
+    return h.escape(str(text).replace('&', '&amp;').replace('<', '&lt;').replace('>', '&gt;'))
